New boundary finding, routed to the batch-30 owner: `lem-cg-davis-angular-vertex-link-is-metric-flag-nerve` Statement (3), Proof 1.6 and gluing step 2.2 use `Σ(B_T)` for every spherical T and `Σ(B_U)` for every U⊆T, including the empty set. The cited Gram definition starts with `n≥0` and an `(n+1)×(n+1)` matrix; the Gram lemma (i),(ii) inherits that nonempty domain. No explicit `Σ(B_∅)` convention was recovered. Its angular-link definition already supplies the correct empty geometry: a zero-dimensional point cell has direction space `{0}`, tangent cone `{0}` and no unit vector, so its link is empty. The Gram lemma (iii) also explicitly realizes the empty complex as empty, but does not name an empty-matrix simplex. This is a nonfatal notation/citation-domain caveat, not a false empty-link conclusion or a missing geometric theorem. Positive-rank Gram and Schur-complement uses are reviewed separately.

Smallest remedy: in the existing consumer state the T=∅ and U=∅ links directly as empty and restrict the cited Gram-construction use to nonempty sets, or explicitly define the empty-Gram notation without extending the nonempty hemisphere/Lipschitz assertions to an empty domain. This scope-denial task licenses artifacts and coverage decisions; its optional existing-item supervising repair task is absent. Existing-item editing is therefore outside this dispatch's write scope.
